# Deliver the canonical Mad Gab resegmentations from approximate mode

## Goal

`madgab --approximate` should propose `wreck a nice beach` for
`recognize speech` and `Hits Justice Dupe Hid Came` for
`It's just a stupid game`, as a result of general improvements to
search, segmentation, scoring and ranking — never phrase-specific
hard-coding.

## Context

The itinerary's primary goal is
[../skills/itinerary-madgab.md](../skills/itinerary-madgab.md). The two
regression tests in `tests/corpus_integration.rs`
(`approximate_finds_recognize_speech_resegmentation`,
`approximate_finds_classic_madgab_resegmentation`) have never passed on
this branch.

Measured starting point on `post-milestone-acceptance` (commit 247404f):

- both desired word sequences are already reachable in the fuzzy matcher
  lattice inside the default budgets; `acceptance_sequences_are_reachable_in_fuzzy_lattice`
  in `src/lib.rs` proves it;
- `wreck a nice beach` was generated at raw rank 662 of 14068
  candidates, score 0.8946, against a 0.9063 cutoff — a ranking problem;
- `hits justice dupe hid came` was **not generated at all**; its
  best alignment costs 1.12 (hits 0.20, justice 0.00, dupe 0.15,
  hid 0.37, came 0.40) inside the 1.5 total budget, but the word `hid`
  was pruned out of the retained shortlist for its target span, and the
  segmentation itself did not survive the structural DP;
- the visible top-50 was pathologically redundant: for
  `It's just a stupid game`, 27 of the 50 proposals shared one boundary
  structure, because `select_diverse` scaled its redundancy penalty by
  the score spread *inside the requested top-N* (a few thousandths), and
  `dedup` treated `this` and `this'` as different clues;
- wall clock was 9.5s for `recognize speech` and 21.3s for
  `It's just a stupid game`, 48.5s for
  `congratulations on your promotion`.

## Completion criteria

- Both canonical clues appear in the approximate proposal set for their
  own target, with the rank chosen by evidence rather than asserted
  arbitrarily; a regression test states the boundary that actually
  matters and the reasoning.
- The implementation contains no phrase-specific special case for these
  examples, their words, or any substring of them.
- The proposal set is not redundant: proposals for one target span
  several distinct boundary structures, covered by a general test.
- `cargo test --release` is green.
- Search wall clock does not regress against the figures above.
- Work is committed on a focused branch and integrated into
  `post-milestone-acceptance`, never into `main`.

## Handoff / notes

Branch `madgab-approx-acceptance` (commit 6250ba3) lands the first slice,
by Antonina agent `a1b2c301`:

- the score's raw-IPA-length proxy is replaced by a syllable-count
  *rhythm* axis (weight 0.30), so the search no longer wins by shredding
  the target into one- and two-phone words;
- clue identity is now orthography-independent (`phrase_signature`), so
  spelling variants of one clue collapse;
- `select_diverse` measures its penalty against the whole candidate
  pool, maximises redundancy over everything picked rather than only the
  previous pick, and adds a 0.5-weighted boundary-cut Jaccard term;
- the per-segmentation lexical enumeration became a best-first search
  over prefixes with admissible suffix bounds, so emission order really
  is descending in final score;
- the per-span shortlist gained a least-familiar-candidate axis and a
  160-word fill, and segmentation retention doubled to 256;
- beam retention is now deterministic (deduped candidates sorted by path
  key, cell keys sorted) — `HashMap` iteration order had been leaking
  into results;
- `boundary_novelty` counts by merge instead of building `HashSet`s, and
  the target-word reuse test is memoised.

Measured result: `wreck a nice beach` raw rank 662 -> 52
(score 0.9277 against a 0.9279 cutoff) and *not* in the visible top 120
either, because MMR keeps at most one clue per boundary structure and two
siblings of the same structure already outscore it; top-50 structural
redundancy is gone; search is 3-5x faster
(`congratulations on your promotion` 48.5s -> 11.5s).

Residual, and the reason this item is still `working`:

- `hits justice dupe hid came` is still absent from the retained pool.
  It scores 0.820 against a 0.926 cutoff: it loses on acoustic cost
  (-0.057), boundary novelty (-0.050) and familiarity (-0.030) and wins
  only on the two weak axes, so no monotone reweighting of the current
  axes can surface it.
- The current winners are function-word salads — `it justice two bad
  aim`, `wreck a nice each`, `so la cut are -able` — that no human would
  use as a puzzle. A closed-class / content-word prior on clue quality is
  the honest next axis, and it is expected to help both canonical cases.
  Tracked as [w-9c2d51](w-9c2d51.md).

integrated tree (~46s). `main` untouched. That front's validation of the
rest of the suite: `--lib` 29/29, `--test exact_determinism` 1/1,
`--test corpus_integration` 8 passed / 1 failed.

The real result of this pass is a **contradiction between two fronts
about the same clue on the same head**, which is more useful than either
measurement alone:

- [w-04f83f](w-04f83f.md), the scorer front, has been measuring for half
  an hour that all five canonical words survive the per-span shortlist
  and that the alignment is lost to the objective — 0.819901 against a
  0.929514 cutoff — and has been sweeping axis weightings accordingly.
- The determinism front, which could not touch `src/` and therefore
  measured the *real* release binary instead, finds the phrase absent
  from the **deduplicated candidate pool** even at `--top 2000`, and
  that `dupe` and `hid` never occur in any visible list at all while
  `hits` and `came` do.

No reweighting can surface a word the matcher never returns, so at most
one of these is right, and the second is the cheaper explanation. The
most likely reconciliation is that the scorer front's reachability
evidence came from a harness with a wider budget than the default path
— which is exactly the mistake its own item warned about, so the
contradiction is a warning about evidence quality rather than a reason
to discard the front.

Actions taken:

- opened [w-7b41d2](w-7b41d2.md) to settle the dispute by measurement,
  claimed it, and assigned agent `e5a71b` in
  `/workspace/madgab-canon-reach` (branch `madgab-canon-reach`, clean
  tree from `76c693c`, `src/` writable). Its first deliverable is
  diagnosis — the exact point in `FuzzyLexicon::matches_at` under the
  *default* configuration where `dupe` and `hid` fail to enter the
  returned shortlist — plus a bisect against `9ae1f5a`, which may show
  the reach front itself is what stopped them being returned.
- steered the running `04f83f0` with the conflicting measurement, the
  reasoning above, and a request to re-verify reachability against the

This item stays `working`, and its blocker is now correctly described:
not "one agent away" but **one disputed measurement away**. The first
canonical case is still generated and visible; the second is claimed by

until that is settled no scorer change can be reviewed on its merits.

## Pass 2026-09-26T14:10Z (coordinator coord-6f0a): the dispute is settled, by measurement

The blocker on this item is no longer disputed. The coordinator re-ran the
release binary built from the current head `434312e` on the **default**
approximate path:

```text
$ MADGAB_TRACE_PHRASES="hits justice dupe hid came" \
    ./target/release/madgab --approximate --top 200 "It's just a stupid game"
MADGAB_TRACE raw phrase="hits justice dupe hid came" missing candidates=17405
MADGAB_TRACE raw_cutoff rank=199 score=0.906262887 phrase="it justice too uhh dame"
(corpus loaded in 499ms; search 3007ms)
```

The canonical alignment is absent from the **deduplicated candidate pool**, not
merely from the visible list, exactly as [w-3d6c81](w-3d6c81.md) reported.
That falsifies [w-04f83f](w-04f83f.md)'s premise — that the five words
survive the shortlist and the clue is lost to the objective at 0.819901
against 0.929514 — because a candidate that is never enumerated cannot be
reweighted into the pool. So the last blocker is **lexical reachability inside
`FuzzyLexicon::matches_at`**, it is owned by [w-7b41d2](w-7b41d2.md), and no
scoring change can be reviewed on its merits until that is fixed. This item's
blocker list is unchanged in count and changed in kind.

## Pass 2026-09-26T15:20Z (coordinator coord-9c17): the last blocker is enumeration only, now measured

No source change on this pass and nothing integrated. `post-milestone-acceptance`
is at `e44269a` and pushed; `main` untouched. Suite state re-measured on
that head by the coordinator, not copied forward:

```text
cargo test --release --test corpus_integration   8 passed, 1 failed
  approximate_finds_classic_madgab_resegmentation  FAILED (still)
  the visible list for its target is still "it justice too <X> aim" wordings
```

The useful result of this pass is a measurement that removes the last
reason to doubt the standing plan. The previous passes treated
"the canonical clue scores 0.8199 against a 0.9157 visible cutoff" as
evidence that it could not surface without a scoring change. That is not
how the selector works, and the following measurement, taken on `e44269a`
through the public API with `madgab::Clue::cuts`, shows it:

```text
target                        top_n  visible  distinct_structures  max_per_structure
It's just a stupid game          50       50            6                     17
It's just a stupid game          20       20            4                      7
recognize speech                 50       50            5                     17
recognize speech                 20       20            3                      7
a whole lot of trouble           50       50            6                     15
a whole lot of trouble           20       20            5                      6
```

17 is exactly `share_cap(50, available) = 50 / STRUCTURE_FLOOR(3)` and 7 is
`share_cap(20, available)`, so the share cap is binding in every case.
Step 1 of `select_diverse` admits one representative per structure from
inside the score cutoff; because the cutoff holds only 5-6 distinct
structures it fills 5-6 slots, not all 50, so **step 2 runs and walks the
whole deduplicated pool**, admitting any candidate whose structure is
unrepresented or under its cap. The visible list is therefore not decided
by the score cutoff at all.

Consequences, which are what `e5a71b` was steered with:

- getting the canonical clue into the **pool** is sufficient. It does not
  have to outscore anything, and no axis weight needs to move — which also
  means the fix will not re-baseline `approximate_output_is_locked` and
  will not make [w-2e5b93](w-2e5b93.md)'s measurement ambiguous;
- the one question left is whether the canonical segmentation's cuts are
  one of the 6 visible structures at its 17-cap, which the agent can check
  in a minute with the same reading of `Clue::cuts`;
- the itinerary's completion criterion — the requested clue appears in the
  generated proposal set — is reachable by enumeration alone, so this item
  stays **one integration away from done** and no further.

Both running agents were left running:

- `e5a71b` ([w-7b41d2](w-7b41d2.md), critical path) — steered with the
  table above, with the instruction not to touch `axes::` weights, with the
  remaining cap question, and with the cost model: the recovery
  enumeration pushes ~8.9M heap branches per search, and `d17a620`'s
  unreviewed branch makes that same enumeration ~39% cheaper, so a wider
  fan-out has a concrete offsetting win to cite — while not assuming it is
  integrated.
- `d17a620` ([w-d17a62](w-d17a62.md)) — nudged for durability only: its
  rebased `889923e` was unpushed and is now pushed, and it is finishing the
  bit-identity re-measurement against `3d0130a`.

**`e5a71b` produced the pass's most useful result, and it is a negative one.**
Its cost sweep says the obvious fix is not available: dropping the per-slot cap
entirely takes 135 s against a 2.0 s budget, and an incumbent-bar variant
takes 35 s and still misses. The general finding it distilled is the real
blocker, and it is worth stating in the item's own words because it retires the
whole family of "just widen it" fixes:

> a best-first enumeration with a per-slot cap can only ever find wordings
> that are near-optimal *at every slot simultaneously*; the canonical is
> 3rd/2nd/10th/99th/11th, so it needs the fan-out to reach rank 99 at slot 3,
> and for a 160-wide shortlist that is 160^5 combinations.

So the last blocker is not "the cap is too small", it is the *shape* of a
single-segmentation best-first product enumeration. The budget the previous
passes hoped would pay for a wider fan-out now genuinely exists — the
integrated commit makes that same enumeration ~39% cheaper — but at 135 s for
full fan-out it is not enough on its own, and `e5a71b` has been told to
re-measure the curve on `7faa74c` rather than on its stale `3d0130a` base
before it claims the fix is unaffordable.

  branch on this host at all, local or remote, so the "never merge
  scheduled work into `main`" condition holds trivially and the
  accumulation branch is unambiguous.
- Both working fronts are on a *named branch*, not a detached HEAD — the
  durability hazard called out in the 15:10Z pass is not present on either
  (`madgab-canon-reach` at `7faa74c`, `madgab-enum-portfolio` at
  `c191eaf`). Neither branch has unmerged commits. `madgab-canon-reach`
  does have an uncommitted instrumented `src/lib.rs` and
  `madgab-enum-portfolio` an untracked `tests/zz_scratch_analysis.rs`;
  both were told explicitly that these must not reach the accumulation
  branch.
- Suite re-measured by this coordinator on `ac58702`, not copied forward:
  `cargo test --release --lib` 31/31; `--test corpus_integration
  approximate_finds` 1 passed / 1 failed, the failure being this item's
  target, whose top-50 is still
  `["it justice too bad aim", "it justice too pad aim", ...,
  "it justice too bad came"]`. Note `came` *is* reachable, which is
  consistent with the per-slot rank table below.
- A correction to durable state: the `updated` fields in these work items
  have been running several hours ahead of real time (this pass is
  11:52Z, the previous pass's item claims 16:20Z, and the real commit
  times are 11:39Z-11:46Z). Timestamps in this repository are therefore
  not a reliable signal of recency or abandonment; use commit times and
  `antonina agent status` instead.

**The useful result of this pass is a reframing of the blocker that both
fronts can act on immediately.** The previous passes have been treating
"the per-slot cap of 10 is too small" as the blocker. The per-slot rank
table already in [w-7b41d2](w-7b41d2.md) says the canonical alignment sits
at per-slot ranks **3 / 2 / 10 / 99 / 11** over the 160-wide shortlist.
Four of the five slots are already inside a cap of 11; exactly one slot —
the `hid` slot, at rank 99 — is out of reach. So the clue does not require
simultaneous deviation across slots, which is what
[the 15:50Z pass](w-4b1e07.md) concluded the best-first product search
could not do. It requires **one slot to be allowed much deeper than the
others**, and the reason it cannot get that is that
`LEXICAL_BRANCH_KEEP` is a single constant applied uniformly to every slot
(`src/lib.rs:1089`). The uniform cap *is* the simultaneous-near-optimality
requirement; the fix is to make the cap per-slot and budget-derived rather
than to raise it.

That is also consistent with the second measurement, taken from
`e5a71b`'s own log on `7faa74c`: widening the uniform cap **shrinks** the
candidate pool, because the per-segmentation pop limit binds.

```text
fanout=10   2309ms  pool 12358
fanout=16   2900ms  pool  9017
fanout=32   4871ms  pool  6994
fanout=64   7858ms  pool  5890
fanout=160 17238ms  pool  5366
fanout=160 emit=256   16884ms  pool 10889
fanout=160 emit=4096  17644ms  pool 26993
```

So "raise `LEXICAL_BRANCH_KEEP`" is refuted by measurement rather than
merely priced out, which closes the last "just widen it" door in the
direction the earlier passes were pushing it. Because emission cost is
additive and already compared against `total_budget` at `src/lib.rs:1063`,
per-slot affordability is directly computable: with `m_j` the minimum cost
in slot `j`, slot `k` can admit candidate `i` whenever
`sum_{j != k} m_j + cost_{k,i} <= total_budget`. This is a cap derived
from the bound the search already respects, it widens precisely the slot
where budget slack exists, and it is nearly free in the common case
because most slots can only afford a handful of alternatives.

Both agents were steered with this, non-blockingly, and left running. The
separation of the two fronts was made explicit in both directions, so they
compose instead of duplicating:

- `e5a71b` ([w-7b41d2](w-7b41d2.md)) — owns the **per-slot cap shape**.
  Given the widening table, the 3/2/10/99/11 reading, and the
  affordability inequality; told explicitly *not* to implement a global
  budget and not to change `LEXICAL_COMBINATIONS_PER_SEGMENTATION` or the
  pop limit; told that if its change needs a global bound to stay
  affordable it should say so in the item and let the coordinator sequence
  the two.
- `6b2f04` ([w-6b2f04](w-6b2f04.md)) — owns the **global budget across
  segmentations**. Given the widening table, the selector measurement from
  the 15:20Z pass (the visible list is not decided by the score cutoff, so
  pool membership is sufficient and no weight needs to move), and the
  asymmetry that is its justification: 256 retained segmentations are
  funded a full emission budget each while the selector can only ever show
  5-6 distinct boundary structures. Told to quantify that asymmetry itself
  rather than assume it, told *not* to implement a per-slot cap change, and
  told that its first measurement ran in Exact mode and every number must

`docs/work/items/w-8f3c61.md`, so `src/` is byte-identical and nothing in
it can be phrase-specific; the item's state moves `working` -> `done` on

family. The branch is pushed (`origin/madgab-depth-order` = `af56ecd`).

what the merge carries is the *refutation*, which is now on the branch a
pass reads by default rather than on a child branch it has to know about.

**That refutation is what this pass is really about, because it closes the
enumeration side of this item in closed form.** §8 of that item, measured
and re-verified from a confirmed build: the canonical tuple needs **>=
404,081 emissions** of its own segmentation to be emitted, the cheapest
depth-profile stratification is bounded below by **134,400 emissions**
(the coordinate-wise down-set, i.e. the best case over all stratified
schedules), against a per-segmentation allowance of **64** and a whole-search
global budget of **16,384**. So the residual cannot be bought with a
selection rule or a visit order at any affordable budget. Its §8.3 names
the only two remaining levers, and both were fenced when it was written:
change what the enumeration optimises, or grow the budget ~8x.

**New front: [w-3b8e15](w-3b8e15.md)**, claimed and assigned to agent
`3b8e15` in `/workspace/madgab-fuzzy-cost` (branch `madgab-fuzzy-cost`,
from `1ff73bb`, pushed). It takes the first lever, and it is the one thing
every previous front was barred from touching: **the quantity the
enumeration optimises** — the fuzzy matcher's own cost. The canonical clue
`hits justice dupe hid came` is a real, near-exact homophonic
resegmentation, yet the matcher charges it `hits 0.20, justice 0.00, dupe
0.15, hid 0.37, came 0.40`, total **1.12** against a `total_budget` of
1.5. In a correct cost model a homophone is nearly free, and because the
traversal is best-first in exactly that cost, overcharging it is what
buries it at emission 23 962 — a lower cost moves the clue into the
traversal-s cheapest corner and reaches it inside the budgets the search
already respects, with no budget, order, width or scoring change at all.
The mechanism is general by construction: a phonemic-confusability
substitution cost derived from the corpus alphabet instead of
`phonetics::distance` between two single IPA symbols, and/or a folding of
the IPA on both sides before comparison. It is fenced to `src/approx.rs`
so it does not contend with the two live fronts, both of which edit
`src/lib.rs`. Unlike every earlier front, a cost change is expected to
re-baseline `approximate_output_is_locked`; the item says so explicitly
and requires a justified before/after table rather than a silent
re-baseline.

### Record correction that a later pass must not miss

The `5f1c04` numbers that were in flight when the previous pass wrote its record,
**and that are still quoted in [w-c1d3a7](w-c1d3a7.md)**, are **superseded
artefacts of a buggy build** and must not be carried forward:

| | superseded (buggy build) | confirmed (clean build, `f2746f5`) |
|---|---|---|
| whole-search emissions at `ZZ_DEEPCAP` 131,072 | 80,367 | **145,247** |
| at 262,144 | 80,367 | **276,319** |
| "saturation" | ~66,176 | **none — the traversal never saturates** |

The bug was a `LEXICAL_GLOBAL_EMISSION_BUDGET` reference split across two lines
that a string-level override missed, so the real 16,384 ceiling was still live at
one traversal exit and the "saturation point" was that ceiling rather than a
property of the traversal. The **conclusion is unchanged** — the clue is absent at
every depth, 4,096 / 16,384 / 65,536 / 131,072 / 262,144 / 1,000,000 / 2,000,000
— but the mechanism is different and stronger: at 2,000,000 the traversal has
covered 0.075% of its own 2,666,496,000-leaf product, and the wall is
`LEXICAL_BRANCH_STAGE_0 = 10` plus drain-before-widen, not a budget.
`w-c1d3a7`'s owner has been told to correct its own file; this entry exists
because that file is mid-edit by a running agent and must not be written by a

`b4e8d1` pushed `1992b76` on `madgab-review-b4e8d1` (docs only, 726 insertions):
the **no-hard-coding audit of `src/` and `tests/` on `aa453c1` is clean**, with

Its fence review of the adjacency WIP found two real mechanism defects and filed

`working` until the agent process exits; a later pass flips it to `done` with the

### The adjacency front closed as a refutation, and the refutation is sound

`c1d3a7` pushed `413113a`: `src/` unchanged from `9767caf`, plus a 243-line
close-out. The result is the most useful thing this repository has produced in
several passes, because it is a *negative* result with numbers:

* the second canonical clue's own alignment **is in a retained pool** — slot
  indices `[7, 0, 13, 99, 11]`, per-word costs totalling 1.120 inside the 1.5
  budget;
* **injected and re-scored, its real final score is 0.819901291, at raw rank
  8,967 of 17,553.** A perfect enumerator still would not show it. So for this
  clue the order is score-then-reach, not reach-then-score, and no further
  reach work can close the target;
* the axis table is the actionable part: the clue is strictly **worse** than the
  cutoff-setting clue on *boundary novelty* (0.667 vs 1.000) and *word
  familiarity* (0.399 vs 0.768), equal on novelty and rhythm, and strictly
  *better* on the acoustic aggregate. It loses on structure and commonness, not
  on pronunciation;
* the first canonical condition is **preserved**: `wreck a nice beach` for
  `recognize speech` at raw rank 27, score 0.918313383, identical with and
  without the operator. Not a trade;
* `SEGMENTATION_KEEP` = 2,048 was measured on a scratch branch and **rejected**:
  pool 56,697, 16.6 s, clue still absent. Consistent with `w-be6d21` and
  `w-5f1c04`; the reach families are closed for good.

Suites: `--lib` 46 passed; `corpus_integration` 9 passed / 1 failed (the known
blocker, output verbatim in the close-out); `exact_determinism` 1;
`approx_determinism` 2. No fmt/clippy on this host.

The coordinator checked the two claims that would have invalidated it and both
hold: `SEGMENTATION_KEEP` is 256 on the branch (the 2,048 was scratch-only), and
the decisive measurement is **injection-based**, so it does not depend on the
adjacency walk admitting anything. Defect 1 in `w-6f3a91` — `pops` spent on
seeds, so the operator is a no-op whenever `|pooled| >= pops`, which is most
segmentations — therefore makes the operator *worth less* than its close-out
claims, and leaves the score-bound conclusion exactly as true. The five
`// ZZ_SCRATCH` markers are committed at `413113a` (`src/lib.rs` lines 1055,
1316, 1699, 1740, 1755) but are bare comments with no instrumentation behind
them. So: `w-6f3a91` is claimed for `c1d3a7` with a cleanup commit as its next
action, and **`madgab-adjacency` is not proposed for integration in this pass** —
a branch carrying a comment that contradicts its own constant is not a
reviewable integration candidate. Its measured win on
`congratulations on your promotion` (two wordings at 0.888 entering at ranks 3-4,
no target's list worse, pool +0.8..12.9%, wall clock within noise) is preserved
on the branch for a later pass to land after the cleanup.

### The conflict this pass found, and the front it produced

`413113a` §8.3 concludes the clue "is cut, by 0.0958" against the visible cutoff
and names "the visible cutoff" as a lever. [w-2e5b93](w-2e5b93.md)'s status note
says the opposite — the list is not decided by the cutoff, because step 2 walks
the whole pool. Reading `select_diverse` on this head shows **both are
incomplete**: step 2 is `for &i in &order` over the whole pool, gated only by
`counts[structure] < cap` with `cap = share_cap(top_n, available.len())` and
`available` the pool-wide structure count. So a below-cutoff candidate *is*
reachable, and what actually binds is the candidate's **rank within its own
boundary structure** against that cap. The first claim treats step 1's bound as
if it governed the whole list; the second never names the within-structure rank
that does.

That is a precise, testable, and *general* question — it is about how selection
composes a global score order with a per-structure quota, not about any
sentence — so it is filed as [w-7b2d40](w-7b2d40.md), claimed in the same push
that created it, and assigned to a new agent `7b2d40` in
`/workspace/madgab-capfront` on branch `madgab-cap-front-7b2d40`. Its first
criterion is to *measure* the within-structure rank distribution and the cap on
six targets and report the share of below-cutoff candidates that are visible. If
that share is already healthy, it closes as a refutation with numbers, and the
score deficit goes to `w-2e5b93` as a new item instead. Fences: no `axes::*`
weight sweep (that question is already owned and a sweep was refuted once), and
nothing in `src/adjacency.rs`.

`w-2e5b93` stays `open` and stays gated, and the gate is now *sharper*: its
per-axis spread table must be re-taken on a settled pool, and the pool is now
settled in the only sense that matters — the reach families are closed and the
residual is score and selection. It starts after `w-7b2d40` reports, not before,
and only if the measurement puts the residual on the score side.

### Deliberately not done

No integration of `madgab-adjacency` (cleanup owed, see above). No weight
sweep. No change to `w-a02d28` or `w-04f83f`, whose unblock conditions are
objective and unchanged. No attempt to close this item: the milestone condition
on the second example is still unmet, and closing it on the strength of a
refutation would be exactly the "done" that is not done.

### Next action for a later fresh pass, in priority order

**The pass-s headline is that [w-c4d7e8](w-c4d7e8.md) reversed the blocker.**

from the deduplicated candidate pool*. Agent `c4d7e8`, which reached

directly: of 1,545,903 enumerated five-word alignments, **40,546 contain
`dupe`**, the best of them scoring 0.8834 at rank 130. The canonical answer
is in the pool and **ranks out** at 0.7999 against a 0.8978 rank-50 cutoff.

*objective*, and it means every "it is not in the pool" sentence in this
item's history is now known to be false. The audit also closes the whole
cost family with an upper bound that does not depend on how the pool is
enumerated: a zero-cost, perfect pronunciation of the canonical wording still
scores **0.8699 < 0.8978**, so no dictionary, `GAP_COST`, per-word
cost-model or substitution-weighting change can put it in the top 50.

### Why `w-9c6f2b` exists, in one paragraph

`c4d7e8` priced the two remaining levers and they are individually
insufficient and jointly sufficient, which is the whole reason the item is
open rather than handed to [w-a02d28](w-a02d28.md) as a product decision.
`SIMILARITY` divides the total substitution cost by the constant `4` rather
than by word count or target length (`src/lib.rs:2377`), a general
per-word bias worth at most **+0.0700** here. `boundary_novelty` is pure
Jaccard (`src/lib.rs:2564`), so a clue that preserves one target word
boundary in five is docked **-0.0500** - it punishes exactly the property a
Mad Gab resegmentation is supposed to have - and unbinding that is worth at
most **+0.0500**. Joint headroom **+0.1200** against a **+0.0979** gap. The
item records the caveat that decides how it must be worked: the cutoff is
*relative*, so raising similarity for every long clue raises the cutoff too,
the criterion is the **rank in the printed set** and never an absolute
score, and if both fixes land and it still ranks out that is a valuable
result to record rather than a third weight move to attempt.

### Blocker count, unchanged at one

`approximate_finds_classic_madgab_resegmentation`. Its *kind* changed: it is
no longer an enumeration or cost problem, it is an objective-relative-ranking
problem, and that is now owned by [w-9c6f2b](w-9c6f2b.md) instead of by a
spent enumeration front.

The local-vs-remote branch reconciliation that four consecutive passes have
been asked to run found one, and it is a **false negative worth recording**:
the reconciliation only checks whether a branch *name* is present on the
remote, so it reported `madgab-integrate-queue` as pushed. Its remote tip was
in fact the **pre-rebase** `057840d` while the local branch had been rebased

`8525123`, `3ae248c`, `89163dd`). Those five commits — the whole integration —
existed on exactly one worktree. A later pass must therefore compare **shas**,
not names:

```sh
git ls-remote --heads origin > /tmp/lsr.txt
node -e 'const fs=require("fs"),cp=require("child_process");
const r=new Map(fs.readFileSync("/tmp/lsr.txt","utf8").split("\n").filter(Boolean)
  .map(l=>l.split("\t")).map(([s,n])=>[n.replace("refs/heads/",""),s]));
for(const l of cp.execSync("git for-each-ref --format=\"%(refname:short) %(objectname)\" refs/heads/").toString().split("\n").filter(Boolean)){
  const i=l.lastIndexOf(" "),b=l.slice(0,i),s=l.slice(i+1);
  if(!r.has(b)) console.log("ABSENT:  "+b);
  else if(r.get(b)!==s) console.log("BEHIND:  "+b+" local="+s.slice(0,7)+" remote="+r.get(b).slice(0,7));
}'
```

`origin/main`'s `c0ecd7c` is asserted again after this push.

The rebased stack was additionally preserved as a net at
`refs/heads/wip/integrate-queue-rebased-89163dd` before it was fast-forwarded,

### What the surviving front has already found, and the one thing worth watching

`9c6f2b` has committed the first half of its mandate and rebased it onto
`89163dd` without conflict. On the **first** of its two levers, the
`SIMILARITY` divisor, it landed a general per-word reading of the axis (the
axis divided a candidate's *total* edit cost by the constant `4.0`, so a clue
paid once for a per-word property and two clues with equally good words were
ordered by word count) and deliberately left the one-word span proxies on their
old weight, recording that re-deriving all three proxy sites on the new axis's
exact marginal weight `-SIMILARITY/(words+1)` was **measured** to lose
`approximate_pool_reaches_matches_deep_in_a_span` — an enumeration-side
effect, fenced out of this front. That is the right call and the right
reasoning: it prices a blocked lever instead of smuggling an enumeration
change in under a scoring front.

On the **second** lever it reports a refutation with a proof rather than a
sweep: the Jaccard distance is never below either one-sided reading of the
same resegmentation, so no redefinition that rewards boundary *addition*
without punishing boundary *preservation* can raise a sharing clue's score, and
the 4x-consistent proxy variant that would have moved the canonical `dupe`
candidate from rank 6328 to 1917 is the very variant that loses the reach
guard. If that holds up under review, the objective-axis family narrows to a
single lever, and the honest next front is a selector one — the
`select_diverse` structure share cap — not a third weight move. That is
`w-9c6f2b`'s call to finish and a coordinator's to review; this pass does not
pre-empt it.

silent — `agent new` succeeds and reports `idle`, so the failure only appears
on `agent prompt`. A later pass that does not export first will burn three
launches the same way; check `agent status` for `exit code 127` rather than
assuming a launch worked.

### The host fact, restated because it cost this pass its first several minutes

`docs/environment-notes.md` says `PATH` is truncated rather than the tools being
absent, and it is right — but the concrete form is worth having verbatim, since
the symptom is actively misleading. On this host `git`, `ls`, `cat`, `head`,
`grep` and `sed` are all absent from the default `PATH`, and the fix is:

```sh
export PATH="$GUIX_PROFILE/bin:$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"
```

without which `git status` returns `git: not found` and the whole repository
appears to have no VCS at all. Export it **before** `antonina agent new`, not
merely before using git or cargo. Note that `PATH` did not get truncated *for
Antonina's children* in the end — the recorded agent failures
(`b3e91a`-`b3e91d`, `exit code 127`, `"OpenCode process had no pid"`) were
launched by a pass that had not exported, and the four passes since have had no
such failures. If a future pass sees `exit code 127` on launch, check this
before assuming anything about the agent.

"already has a durable `wip/*` copy at the local sha", naming four of them. **None
of those four `wip/*` refs existed.** Before this pass, `refs/heads/wip/` held
exactly two entries, neither of them one of the four:

```text
wip/c1d3a7-cleanup-7d3f1
wip/enum-portfolio-6b2f04
```

Sorting the six reported lines by whether the local tip is already an ancestor of
`post-milestone-acceptance` gives a much more useful answer than "IN SYNC /
BEHIND / ABSENT", and it splits them cleanly:

- `madgab-integrate-queue` (local `89163dd`) and `madgab-objective-axes` (local
  `f71b634`) are **already ancestors of `post-milestone-acceptance`** with **0**
  commits each not in it. They are `BEHIND` only because the remote copies are
  stale divergent lines. **No hazard at all**, and none needed closing.
- `scratch/9c6f2b-harness` (`d4991dd`, **3** commits not in the accumulation
  branch) and `scratch/9c6f2b-probe` (`e9a2797`, **4** commits not in it) are
  **genuine single-copy hazards**, because their unique commits are the
  `ZZ_PHRASES` / `ZZ_STRUCT` measurement harness from the retired w-9c6f2b
  front. Small and throwaway, but real.

Closed for real this time, by creating the refs and verifying they resolve:

```text
wip/9c6f2b-harness-d4991dd        d4991dd   3 commits now pinned
wip/9c6f2b-probe-e9a2797          e9a2797   4 commits now pinned
wip/scratch-b3e91a-probe-4f3442c  4f3442c   ABSENT local branch from b3e91a's front
```

`scratch/b3e91a-probe` is new since the last check and is `ABSENT` from the
remote. Its tip is `4f3442c`, which is already durable as
`madgab-d6c-9d4e10`, so it carried no unique work; it is pinned anyway because
`b3e91f` is still running and may advance it. `zzparent` is junk and is left
alone.

**The transferable lesson, which is the same one this repository has now recorded
twice:** a durability check that only reports `IN SYNC` / `BEHIND` / `ABSENT`
cannot distinguish "the local tip is already integrated, so this branch is
irrelevant" from "the local tip has unique commits that exist nowhere else".
Those two cases look identical in the name-only or sha-comparison output and
call for opposite responses. Check `git merge-base --is-ancestor <local-tip>
post-milestone-acceptance`, and when that is false, count
`git rev-list --count post-milestone-acceptance..<local-tip>`. Anything with a
non-zero count needs a `wip/*` ref, created and then verified with
`git rev-parse --verify` before the pass claims it closed.

## Pass 2026-09-27T06:30Z (coordinator coord-9f3c): the objective front closed as a priced negative; the blocker is emission order

The live front from the previous pass finished inside it. Agent `7e1a08` is
`succeeded`, exit 0, `prompts: 4`, and its worktree is clean with three
commits on `madgab-objform-7e1a04`, all on `origin`:

- `b9945e9` and `a645845`, the two pins, reviewed and integrated by the
  previous pass as `3f007db`;
- `3372dbf`, the front's result, **integrated and pushed by this pass** as
  the merge plus `707fb2a`. Review on the diff: the result commit is
  `docs/work/items/w-7e1a04.md` only, 389 added lines, no production line, so
  it cannot move the output lock or the wall clock. The only conflict was that
  one file, and it was resolved by keeping both sides' sections in order.

**The verdict is a priced negative, and it changes what this item's blocker
is.** The hypothesis was that the score's length handling charges a faithful
resegmentation for being finer. In a controlled population of 3,717 faithful
resegmentations (exact mode, `SIMILARITY` exactly 1.000000 on every one, so
only the segmentation varies) the two axes it named *increase* with clue word
count on 10/10 informative targets, pooled `+0.059179` and `+0.021242` raw
per word. `WORD_NOVELTY` is `1 - reused/word_count` entered at `+0.15`, so it
rewards clue words that are *not* target words: the sign of the claim is
backwards. And the arithmetic is decisive on its own — a perfect repair of
the named defect is worth `0.15 * 0.125 = 0.018750` against a gap of
`0.094352` (pool rank-50 cutoff `0.915122`) or `0.092201` (printed-50),
i.e. 20% of the gap, short by a factor of 4.9. The rhythm axis, the last
surviving candidate, references no word count at all: it reads
`|clue_syllables - target_syllables|`, and its controlled slope is exactly
`0.000000` on 5 of 10 informative targets and exactly `-0.500000` (the
one-syllable step) on the other 5.

**So the blocker is emission order, not the score.** The clue is 0 of 18,936
pool members and 0 of 23,296 dumped candidates: it is not in the pool to be
reweighted, which is [w-b3e91a](w-b3e91a.md)'s priced order limit seen from
the other end. This item's blocker count is therefore **unchanged at one**,
`approximate_finds_classic_madgab_resegmentation`, and its kind is settled.

Two durable facts the front also established, which constrain whatever
lands next and are recorded here so they are not re-derived:

* **Length coupling is load-bearing.** 14 of 15 targets admit candidates
  above the rank-50 cutoff in exactly *one* word count, and 10 of 15 visible
  top-50 lists are a single word-count stratum. Flattening the coupling would
  erase the optimum, not shift it, and would flood the visible list with
  4-word function-word salads — which is what the case-2 list already is.
  A change that surfaces the canonical clue by flattening it should be
  rejected on that basis alone.
* **Harness hygiene.** `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` must not be shared between the real
  worktree and a probe worktree; with one shared directory a mutated probe
  build was picked up and `--lib` reported three failures, including two
  pre-existing ones, on a clean tree. Any future number must name its target
  dir.

**New front: [w-5b1e93](w-5b1e93.md)**, claimed, branched
`madgab-emitbound-5b1e93` in `/workspace/madgab-emitbound-5b1e93` from
`707fb2a`, assigned to agent `5b1e930` (**running** at handoff). It is
narrowed to the two uniformities in the lexical traversal of `src/lib.rs` that
produce the order limit:

1. every slot opens at one width, [`LEXICAL_BRANCH_STAGE_0`] = 10, while the
   canonical alignment's per-slot ranks over the 160-wide shortlist are
   **3 / 2 / 10 / 99 / 11** — four slots inside a width of 11 and exactly one
   not, so the clue needs *one* slot to be much deeper than the others;
2. the suffix bound is a **separable sum of independent per-slot extrema**
   (`suf_min_cost`, `suf_min_reused`, `suf_max_fam`, `suf_min_closed`,
   `suf_min_syl`/`suf_max_syl` composed as if one slot could be simultaneously
   cheapest, least-reusing, most familiar, least closed-class and
   syllable-extremal), which is admissible and loose, and a loose bound is
   what makes the walk pass 2,036,664 wordings that cannot beat the clue.

Uniform widening is already refuted by measurement on the current head
(`fanout=10` 2.3 s / pool 12,358 against `fanout=160` 17.2 s / pool 5,366, with
`emit=4096` reaching 26,993 and *still* missing), and reallocating the
emission ceiling is refuted by [w-b3e91a](w-b3e91a.md). The question the new
front asks is ordering quality per unit of budget, not budget size.

[w-7e1a04](w-7e1a04.md) is now `superseded` by `w-5b1e93` rather than
`done`: its criterion 1 was not met, and marking a refuted hypothesis `done`
would tell the next pass the milestone is closed. `main` untouched;
`post-milestone-acceptance` remains the only accumulation branch, at `707fb2a`
and pushed. No production change on this pass.

  uncommitted `src/lib.rs`**.

### Hazard 1 closed: the front's whole change existed in one place

That uncommitted delta is the fifth time this queue has had live work only in
one checkout, and this is the case where losing it would have cost the front
its entire result. It is now durable as **`42075de`** on
`refs/heads/wip/w-5b1e93-inflight-42075de`, pushed, parented on `707fb2a`.

It was built with a scratch `GIT_INDEX_FILE` and `git commit-tree`, so the
running agent's own index and working tree were **not** touched: its
`git status` still reads exactly ` M src/lib.rs` afterwards. This is a
durability net and explicitly **not** a review or a sign-off, and the agent
was told so in the same words.

### The change is general, and its claim is falsifiable

`707fb2a..42075de` is confined to `src/lib.rs` and to the heap's
best-completion bound in the lexical traversal. It replaces
`axes::SIMILARITY * (1.0 - (cost + suf_min_cost[k]) / 4.0)` with the same
expression divided by `n.max(1) as f64 * axes::SIMILARITY_COST_PER_PHONE`.

The claim is a **correctness** claim, not a tuning claim: the bound was still
normalising by a constant `4.0` per *word* after `SIMILARITY` became a
per-*phone* axis, so for any target longer than `4 / 0.30 = 13.3` phones the
bound's penalty is the larger one and the bound sits **below** the score of a
completion it is meant to dominate. That is not an ordering nuisance: the heap
is keyed by this value and the coverage reserve spends its share on "the
highest admissible bound", so an inadmissible key invalidates the argument
that the reserve's choice is the best position it examined. Reading the axis
through the scorer's own normaliser also makes the bound invariant to the cost
model, which is what let the drift arise. No phrase token, no new constant, no
new `env` knob, no `zz*` file — the diff is one expression and one comment.

The agent's own measurement: pool size barely moves, and the rank-50 cutoff
**rises on 13 of 15** real targets and never falls, with the `n=6`/`n=7`
single-stratum optimum preserved on 12 of 15.

`--prompt` refused while the agent was running ("use `--steer`"), so the steer
was reissued as `antonina agent prompt --steer`; it was **taken** — `prompts`

unsteered while believing otherwise.
